<artifact_info>
id: art_UafZp2AqR5at
type: dataset
title: Arithmetic Problems with Checksum Error Labels
summary: >-
  arithmetic_checksum_dataset (full_data_out.json, 1935 rows, 2.9MB, exp_sel_data_out.json-schema-valid) combines two complementary
  sources: 200 real GSM8K word problems (openai/gsm8k, main config, train+test) whose reasoning was auto-parsed via regex
  over the <<operand op operand=result>> calculator annotations into explicit step traces (operand_1, operand_2, operation,
  result, depends_on_step), filtered to chain_length 2-6 and cross-checked so the final trace step matches the stated '####
  answer'; plus 200 procedurally generated synthetic word problems (5 templates: shopping, recipe-scaling, distance-rate-time,
  unit-conversion, inventory-accounting) with traces emitted directly by the generator, guaranteeing clean coverage of long
  chains (5-6 steps) and large numbers (>=100) that GSM8K under-represents. Both sources are stratified across a 5(chain_length)x2(numeric_range)
  grid, 20 items per cell. On top of these 400 base items (metadata_row_type=base_item, input=problem text, output=final answer,
  metadata_trace=full step list), a deterministic error-injection layer adds up to 4 corrupted variants per base item (metadata_row_type=error_variant):
  digit_transposition, dropped_carry, sign_flip, wrong_operand_substitution. Each corruption is injected at one step and propagated
  through every downstream step that depends on it (recomputed via exact arithmetic, not estimated), yielding an internally
  consistent corrupted trace and a corrupted_final_answer; the row's input is the corrupted trace rendered as text and output
  is 'checksum_detectable|correct_final_answer=X' or 'checksum_invisible|correct_final_answer=X' depending on whether the
  mod-9 digit residue of the corrupted final answer differs from (detectable) or matches (invisible) the correct final answer's
  residue. An error type is skipped per item (logged, not force-fit) when it cannot be structurally applied (e.g. sign_flip
  needs a +/- step) or when the corruption doesn't propagate to the final answer (orphan sub-calculation) or has no reachable
  trace; 1935 total rows (400 base + 1535 variants; skip rate ~8%) with an 80/20 metadata_fold train/test split. All arithmetic
  (base traces and every corrupted+propagated trace) was independently re-derived and verified with zero inconsistencies across
  all 1935 rows via a standalone regex-based checker (verify.py) that recomputes every step from the rendered text and cross-checks
  against metadata. Suitable for downstream self-critique experiments testing whether a model's checksum-style spot-check
  catches injected arithmetic errors, and specifically whether it fails on checksum_invisible errors that a naive residue
  check would miss. pyproject.toml pins the sole third-party dependency (loguru==0.7.3); data.py is deterministic (fixed RNG
  seed) and reproducible via `uv run data.py`, reading raw openai/gsm8k JSON from temp/datasets/ (excluded from publish, re-downloadable
  via the aii-hf-datasets skill).

GITHUB_DATA_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AMGrobelnik/ai-invention-74dec2-checksum-self-critique-helps-weak-arithm/main/round-1/dataset-1/demo/mini_demo_data.json"
import json, os

def load_data():
    try:
        import urllib.request
        with urllib.request.urlopen(GITHUB_DATA_URL) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read().decode())
    except Exception: pass
    if os.path.exists("mini_demo_data.json"):
        with open("mini_demo_data.json") as f: return json.load(f)
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not load mini_demo_data.json")
```

generous budget) ----\nRAM_BUDGET = 2 * 1024**3  # 2GB is far more than this workload needs\nresource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS, (RAM_BUDGET * 3, RAM_BUDGET * 3))\n\nWORKSPACE = / \"full_data_out.json\"\n\nRNG = random.Random(20260731)\n\nCALC_RE = re.compile(r\"<<([^=<>]+)=(-?[\\d,]*\\.?\\d+)>>\")\nOP_RE = re.compile(r\"^\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)\\s*([+\\-*/])\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)\\s*$\")\nFINAL_RE = re.compile(r\"####\\s*(-?[\\d,]*\\.?\\d+)\")\n\nEPS = 1e-6\n\n\ndef to_num(s: str):\n    s = s.replace(\",\", \"\")\n    f = float(s)\n    i = int(f)\n    return i if abs(f - i) < EPS else f\n\n\ndef mod9_residue(n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\"\"\"\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\n    if n == 0:\n        return 0\n    r = n % 9\n    return 9 if r == 0 else r\n\n\n# =====================================================================\n# 1) GSM8K real-item trace extraction\n# =====================================================================\n\n\ndef extract_gsm8k_steps(answer_text: str):\n    \"\"\"Parse <<op1 OP op2=result>> annotations into an ordered step list.\n\n    Returns None if any annotation fails to parse as a clean binary op,\n    or if the final #### answer doesn't match the last extracted step.\n    \"\"\"\n    matches = CALC_RE.findall(answer_text)\n    if not matches:\n        return None\n\n    steps = []\n    for step_idx, (expr, result_str) in enumerate(matches):\n        m = OP_RE.match(expr)\n        if not m:\n            return None  # non-binary or malformed annotation -> discard item\n        op1_str, op, op2_str = m.groups()\n        try:\n            op1, op2, result = to_num(op1_str), to_num(op2_str), to_num(result_str)\n        except ValueError:\n            return None\n\n        depends_on = []\n        for prior_idx, prior_step in enumerate(steps):\n            for candidate in (op1, op2):\n                if abs(candidate - prior_step[\"result\"]) < EPS:\n                    depends_on.append(prior_idx)\n                    break\n        steps.append(\n            {\n                \"step_index\": step_idx,\n                \"operand_1\": op1,\n                \"operand_2\": op2,\n                \"operation\": op,\n                \"result\": result,\n                \"depends_on_step\": sorted(set(depends_on)) or None,\n            }\n        )\n\n    final_match = FINAL_RE.search(answer_text)\n    if not final_match:\n        return None\n    try:\n        final_answer = to_num(final_match.group(1))\n    except ValueError:\n        return None\n    if abs(final_answer - steps[-1][\"result\"]) > max(EPS, abs(final_answer) * 1e-4):\n        return None  # trace doesn't lead to the stated final answer -> discard\n\n    return steps, final_answer\n\n\ndef numeric_range_of(steps) -> str:\n    for s in steps:\n        for v in (s[\"operand_1\"], s[\"operand_2\"], s[\"result\"]):\n            if abs(v) >= 100:\n                return \"large\"\n    return \"small\"\n\n\ndef load_gsm8k_base_items(max_per_cell: int = 20):\n    raw = json.loads(GSM8K_TRAIN.read_text()) + json.loads(GSM8K_TEST.read_text())\n    logger.info(f\"Loaded {len(raw)} raw GSM8K rows (train+test combined)\")\n\n    by_cell: dict[tuple[int, str], list[dict]] = {}\n    n_discarded = 0\n    for row_idx, row in enumerate(raw):\n        parsed = extract_gsm8k_steps(row[\"answer\"])\n        if parsed is None:\n            n_discarded += 1\n            continue\n        steps, final_answer = parsed\n        chain_length = len(steps)\n        if not (2 <= chain_length <= 6):\n            n_discarded += 1\n            continue\n        nrange = numeric_range_of(steps)\n        item = {\n            \"item_id\": f\"gsm8k_{row_idx}\",\n            \"problem_text\": row[\"question\"],\n            \"final_answer\": final_answer,\n            \"trace\": steps,\n            \"item_source\": \"gsm8k_real\",\n            \"numeric_range\": nrange,\n            \"chain_length\": chain_length,\n        }\n        by_cell.setdefault((chain_length, nrange), []).append(item)\n\n    logger.info(\n        f\"GSM8K: {sum(len(v) for v in by_cell.values())} clean-parsed items, \"\n        f\"{n_discarded} discarded (unparseable / bad chain_length / trace mismatch)\"\n    )\n\n    selected = []\n    for cell, items in sorted(by_cell.items()):\n        RNG.shuffle(items)\n        take = items[:max_per_cell]\n        selected.extend(take)\n        logger.info(f\"  cell chain_length={cell[0]} numeric_range={cell[1]}: {len(take)}/{len(items)} taken\")\n    return selected\n\n\n# =====================================================================\n# 2) Synthetic template generator (fills gaps GSM8K under-represents:\n#    large numbers, long chains)\n# =====================================================================\n\n\ndef gen_shopping(chain_length: int, nrange: str, item_id: str):\n    \"\"\"Buy N items at unit price, apply repeated discounts/additions.\"\"\"\n    lo, hi = (2, 40) if nrange == \"small\" else (120, 900)\n    price = RNG.randint(lo, hi)\n    qty = RNG.randint(2, 9)\n    steps = []\n    result = price * qty\n    steps.append({\"step_index\": 0, \"operand_1\": price, \"operand_2\": qty, \"operation\": \"*\", \"result\": result, \"depends_on_step\": None})\n    text_parts = [f\"An item costs ${price} and a shopper buys {qty} of them.\"]\n    for i in range(1, chain_length):\n        if i % 2 == 1:\n            extra = RNG.randint(1, max(1, hi // 4))\n            new_result = result + extra\n            text_parts.append(f\"Then the shopper adds ${extra} in shipping/extra fees.\")\n            steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": extra, \"operation\": \"+\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        else:\n            frac = RNG.choice([2, 4, 5])\n            discount = result // frac\n            new_result = result - discount\n            text_parts.append(f\"Then a discount of one-{frac}th of the running total is applied.\")\n            steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": discount, \"operation\": \"-\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        result = new_result\n    text_parts.append(\"What is the final total?\")\n    return \" \".join(text_parts), steps, result\n\n\ndef gen_recipe_scaling(chain_length: int, nrange: str, item_id: str):\n    \"\"\"Scale ingredient amounts across a multi-step recipe conversion chain.\"\"\"\n    lo, hi = (2, 20) if nrange == \"small\" else (110, 800)\n    base_amount = RNG.randint(lo, hi)\n    scale = RNG.randint(2, 6)\n    steps = []\n    result = base_amount * scale\n    steps.append({\"step_index\": 0, \"operand_1\": base_amount, \"operand_2\": scale, \"operation\": \"*\", \"result\": result, \"depends_on_step\": None})\n    text_parts = [f\"A recipe uses {base_amount} grams of an ingredient per batch, and a baker scales it up to {scale} batches.\"]\n    for i in range(1, chain_length):\n        if i % 2 == 1:\n            waste = RNG.randint(1, max(1, hi // 5))\n            new_result = result - waste\n            text_parts.append(f\"{waste} grams are lost to spillage during mixing.\")\n            steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": waste, \"operation\": \"-\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        else:\n            portions = pick_exact_divisor(result, [2, 3, 4])\n            if portions is None:\n                bonus = RNG.randint(1, max(1, hi // 5))\n                new_result = result + bonus\n                text_parts.append(f\"An extra {bonus} grams are added from a backup batch.\")\n                steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": bonus, \"operation\": \"+\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n            else:\n                new_result = result // portions\n                text_parts.append(f\"The batter is then split evenly into {portions} equal portions and only one portion is kept.\")\n                steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": portions, \"operation\": \"/\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        result = new_result\n    text_parts.append(\"How many grams remain in the kept portion?\")\n    return \" \".join(text_parts), steps, result\n\n\ndef gen_distance_rate_time(chain_length: int, nrange: str, item_id: str):\n    \"\"\"Multi-leg trip: distance = rate * time, then combine legs.\"\"\"\n    lo_rate, hi_rate = (3, 15) if nrange == \"small\" else (60, 200)\n    rate = RNG.randint(lo_rate, hi_rate)\n    time_ = RNG.randint(2, 8)\n    steps = []\n    result = rate * time_\n    steps.append({\"step_index\": 0, \"operand_1\": rate, \"operand_2\": time_, \"operation\": \"*\", \"result\": result, \"depends_on_step\": None})\n    text_parts = [f\"A traveler moves at {rate} units per hour for {time_} hours on the first leg of a trip.\"]\n    for i in range(1, chain_length):\n        if i % 2 == 1:\n            leg_rate = RNG.randint(lo_rate, hi_rate)\n            leg_time = RNG.randint(1, 6)\n            leg_dist = leg_rate * leg_time\n            new_result = result + leg_dist\n            text_parts.append(f\"On the next leg they travel {leg_time} more hours at {leg_rate} units per hour.\")\n            steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": leg_dist, \"operation\": \"+\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        else:\n            backtrack = RNG.randint(1, max(1, hi_rate // 2))\n            new_result = result - backtrack\n            text_parts.append(f\"They then backtrack {backtrack} units to retrieve a dropped item.\")\n            steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": backtrack, \"operation\": \"-\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        result = new_result\n    text_parts.append(\"What is the total distance covered?\")\n    return \" \".join(text_parts), steps, result\n\n\ndef gen_unit_conversion(chain_length: int, nrange: str, item_id: str):\n    \"\"\"Chain of unit conversions (e.g. minutes -> hours-equivalent tallies).\"\"\"\n    lo, hi = (5, 90) if nrange == \"small\" else (150, 999)\n    amount = RNG.randint(lo, hi)\n    factor = RNG.choice([2, 3, 4, 5])\n    steps = []\n    result = amount * factor\n    steps.append({\"step_index\": 0, \"operand_1\": amount, \"operand_2\": factor, \"operation\": \"*\", \"result\": result, \"depends_on_step\": None})\n    text_parts = [f\"A worker logs {amount} units of work and a supervisor multiplies it by a conversion factor of {factor}.\"]\n    for i in range(1, chain_length):\n        if i % 2 == 1:\n            bonus = RNG.randint(1, max(1, hi // 3))\n            new_result = result + bonus\n            text_parts.append(f\"A bonus of {bonus} converted units is added.\")\n            steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": bonus, \"operation\": \"+\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        else:\n            div = pick_exact_divisor(result, [2, 3])\n            if div is None:\n                bonus = RNG.randint(1, max(1, hi // 4))\n                new_result = result + bonus\n                text_parts.append(f\"An extra {bonus} converted units are credited.\")\n                steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": bonus, \"operation\": \"+\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n            else:\n                new_result = result // div\n                text_parts.append(f\"The total is then divided evenly among {div} teams and one team's share is reported.\")\n                steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": div, \"operation\": \"/\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        result = new_result\n    text_parts.append(\"What is the final converted amount for that team?\")\n    return \" \".join(text_parts), steps, result\n\n\ndef gen_inventory_accounting(chain_length: int, nrange: str, item_id: str):\n    \"\"\"Multi-step stock in/out ledger.\"\"\"\n    lo, hi = (10, 60) if nrange == \"small\" else (200, 950)\n    stock = RNG.randint(lo, hi)\n    restock = RNG.randint(lo // 2 or 1, hi // 2)\n    steps = []\n    result = stock + restock\n    steps.append({\"step_index\": 0, \"operand_1\": stock, \"operand_2\": restock, \"operation\": \"+\", \"result\": result, \"depends_on_step\": None})\n    text_parts = [f\"A warehouse starts with {stock} units in stock and receives a restock of {restock} units.\"]\n    for i in range(1, chain_length):\n        if i % 2 == 1:\n            sold = RNG.randint(1, max(1, result // 3))\n            new_result = result - sold\n            text_parts.append(f\"Then {sold} units are sold.\")\n            steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": sold, \"operation\": \"-\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        else:\n            crates = pick_exact_divisor(result, [2, 3, 4])\n            if crates is None:\n                sold = RNG.randint(1, max(1, result // 4))\n                new_result = result - sold\n                text_parts.append(f\"Then {sold} more units are sold before repacking.\")\n                steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": sold, \"operation\": \"-\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n            else:\n                new_result = result // crates\n                text_parts.append(f\"The remaining stock is repacked into {crates} equal-sized crates and one crate is shipped out for audit.\")\n                steps.append({\"step_index\": i, \"operand_1\": result, \"operand_2\": crates, \"operation\": \"/\", \"result\": new_result, \"depends_on_step\": [i - 1]})\n        result = new_result\n    text_parts.append(\"How many units are in the audited crate?\")\n    return \" \".join(text_parts), steps, result\n\n\ndef pick_exact_divisor(value: int, candidates: list[int]):\n    \"\"\"Return a candidate divisor that evenly divides value, or None.\"\"\"\n    options = [c for c in candidates if c > 0 and value % c == 0]\n    return RNG.choice(options) if options else None\n\n\nTEMPLATES = [gen_shopping, gen_recipe_scaling, gen_distance_rate_time, gen_unit_conversion, gen_inventory_accounting]\n\n\ndef generate_synthetic_items(per_cell: int = 20):\n    items = []\n    idx = 0\n    for chain_length in range(2, 7):\n        for nrange in (\"small\", \"large\"):\n            for _ in range(per_cell):\n                template = TEMPLATES[idx % len(TEMPLATES)]\n                item_id = f\"synthetic_{idx}\"\n                text, steps, final_answer = template(chain_length, nrange, item_id)\n                # verify constructed trace actually reduces to final_answer\n                assert abs(steps[-1][\"result\"] - final_answer) < EPS, \"generator internal trace mismatch\"\n                items.append(\n                    {\n                        \"item_id\": item_id,\n                        \"problem_text\": text,\n                        \"final_answer\": final_answer,\n                        \"trace\": steps,\n                        \"item_source\": \"synthetic_template\",\n                        \"numeric_range\": numeric_range_of(steps),\n                        \"chain_length\": chain_length,\n                    }\n                )\n                idx += 1\n    logger.info(f\"Synthetic: generated {len(items)} template items across chain_length 2-6 x {{small,large}}\")\n    return items\n\n\n# =====================================================================\n# 3) Error injection (deterministic, pure arithmetic)\n# =====================================================================\n\n\ndef apply_op(op1, op, op2):\n    if op == \"+\":\n        return op1 + op2\n    if op == \"-\":\n        return op1 - op2\n    if op == \"*\":\n        return op1 * op2\n    if op == \"/\":\n        if op2 == 0:\n            return None\n        val = op1 / op2\n        return round(val, 6)\n    raise ValueError(op)\n\n\ndef recompute_full_trace(steps, changed_idx, new_result):\n    \"\"\"Given steps[changed_idx].result replaced by new_result, recompute every\n    downstream step whose operand(s) referenced the (now-changed) upstream\n    result, propagating through the rest of the chain. Returns a list of\n    per-step dicts {operand_1, operand_2, result} for the FULL corrupted\n    trace (all steps, not just downstream ones), or None if recomputation\n    hits an invalid operation (e.g. division by a corrupted zero).\"\"\"\n    orig_results = [s[\"result\"] for s in steps]\n    corrupted = [dict(operand_1=s[\"operand_1\"], operand_2=s[\"operand_2\"], result=s[\"result\"]) for s in steps]\n    corrupted[changed_idx][\"result\"] = new_result\n\n    for i in range(changed_idx + 1, len(steps)):\n        s = steps[i]\n        op1, op2 = s[\"operand_1\"], s[\"operand_2\"]\n        changed = False\n        # any operand that equals a prior step's ORIGINAL result gets swapped\n        # for that prior step's CORRUPTED result if it differs\n        for j in range(i):\n            if abs(orig_results[j] - corrupted[j][\"result\"]) < EPS:\n                continue  # that upstream step wasn't altered, nothing to propagate\n            if abs(op1 - orig_results[j]) < EPS:\n                op1 = corrupted[j][\"result\"]\n                changed = True\n            if abs(op2 - orig_results[j]) < EPS:\n                op2 = corrupted[j][\"result\"]\n                changed = True\n        if not changed:\n            continue\n        new_val = apply_op(op1, s[\"operation\"], op2)\n        if new_val is None:\n            return None\n        corrupted[i] = dict(operand_1=op1, operand_2=op2, result=new_val)\n    return corrupted\n\n\ndef recompute_downstream(steps, changed_idx, new_result):\n    \"\"\"Final corrupted answer after propagating a corruption at changed_idx.\"\"\"\n    full_trace = recompute_full_trace(steps, changed_idx, new_result)\n    if full_trace is None:\n        return None\n    return full_trace[-1][\"result\"]\n\n\ndef err_digit_transposition(steps, step_idx):\n    s = steps[step_idx]\n    result_int = int(round(s[\"result\"]))\n    digits = list(str(abs(result_int)))\n    if len(digits) < 2:\n        return None\n    pos = RNG.randrange(len(digits) - 1)\n    digits[pos], digits[pos + 1] = digits[pos + 1], digits[pos]\n    corrupted = int(\"\".join(digits))\n    if result_int < 0:\n        corrupted = -corrupted\n    if corrupted == result_int:\n        return None\n    return corrupted\n\n\ndef err_dropped_carry(steps, step_idx):\n    s = steps[step_idx]\n    result_int = int(round(s[\"result\"]))\n    if abs(result_int) < 10:\n        return None\n    power = 10 ** RNG.randint(1, max(1, len(str(abs(result_int))) - 1))\n    corrupted = result_int - power\n    if corrupted == result_int:\n        return None\n    return corrupted\n\n\ndef err_sign_flip(steps, step_idx):\n    s = steps[step_idx]\n    if s[\"operation\"] not in (\"+\", \"-\"):\n        return None\n    flipped_op = \"-\" if s[\"operation\"] == \"+\" else \"+\"\n    corrupted = apply_op(s[\"operand_1\"], flipped_op, s[\"operand_2\"])\n    if corrupted is None or abs(corrupted - s[\"result\"]) < EPS:\n        return None\n    return corrupted\n\n\ndef err_wrong_operand(steps, step_idx):\n    s = steps[step_idx]\n    other_vals = [st[\"result\"] for j, st in enumerate(steps) if j != step_idx]\n    other_vals += [st[\"operand_1\"] for j, st in enumerate(steps) if j != step_idx]\n    other_vals = [v for v in other_vals if abs(v - s[\"operand_1\"]) > EPS and abs(v - s[\"operand_2\"]) > EPS]\n    if not other_vals:\n        return None\n    wrong_op2 = RNG.choice(other_vals)\n    corrupted = apply_op(s[\"operand_1\"], s[\"operation\"], wrong_op2)\n    if corrupted is None or abs(corrupted - s[\"result\"]) < EPS:\n        return None\n    return corrupted\n\n\nERROR_FUNCS = {\n    \"digit_transposition\": err_digit_transposition,\n    \"dropped_carry\": err_dropped_carry,\n    \"sign_flip\": err_sign_flip,\n    \"wrong_operand_substitution\": err_wrong_operand,\n}\n\n\ndef render_corrupted_trace(problem_text, steps, full_corrupted_trace):\n    lines = [f\"Problem: {problem_text}\", \"Reasoning trace:\"]\n    for i, s in enumerate(steps):\n        c = full_corrupted_trace[i]\n        lines.append(f\"  Step {i}: {c['operand_1']} {s['operation']} {c['operand_2']} = {c['result']}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef make_error_variants(item, skip_log):\n    variants = []\n    steps = item[\"trace\"]\n    for error_type, fn in ERROR_FUNCS.items():\n        candidate_order = list(range(len(steps)))\n        RNG.shuffle(candidate_order)\n\n        step_idx = corrupted_result = full_corrupted_trace = corrupted_final = None\n        for alt_idx in candidate_order:\n            alt_result = fn(steps, alt_idx)\n            if alt_result is None:\n                continue\n            alt_trace = recompute_full_trace(steps, alt_idx, alt_result)\n            if alt_trace is None:\n                continue\n            alt_final = alt_trace[-1][\"result\"]\n            if abs(alt_final - item[\"final_answer\"]) < EPS:\n                continue  # orphan/parallel sub-calc: corruption doesn't reach the final answer\n            step_idx, corrupted_result, full_corrupted_trace, corrupted_final = alt_idx, alt_result, alt_trace, alt_final\n            break\n\n        if step_idx is None:\n            skip_log.append((item[\"item_id\"], error_type))\n            continue\n\n        residue_correct = mod9_residue(item[\"final_answer\"])\n        residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\n        preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\n        label = \"checksum_invisible\" if preserved else \"checksum_detectable\"\n\n        variants.append(\n            {\n                \"base_item_id\": item[\"item_id\"],\n                \"error_type\": error_type,\n                \"injected_step_index\": step_idx,\n                \"original_step\": dict(steps[step_idx]),\n                \"corrupted_step\": {**steps[step_idx], **full_corrupted_trace[step_idx]},\n                \"full_corrupted_trace\": full_corrupted_trace,\n                \"corrupted_final_answer\": corrupted_final,\n                \"mod9_residue_preserved\": preserved,\n                \"label\": label,\n                \"problem_text\": item[\"problem_text\"],\n                \"trace\": steps,\n                \"item_source\": item[\"item_source\"],\n                \"numeric_range\": item[\"numeric_range\"],\n                \"chain_length\": item[\"chain_length\"],\n            }\n        )\n    return variants\n\n\n# =====================================================================\n# 4) Row rendering (exp_sel_data_out.json schema)\n# =====================================================================\n\n\ndef base_item_to_row(item, fold: int):\n    return {\n        \"input\": item[\"problem_text\"],\n        \"output\": str(item[\"final_answer\"]),\n        \"metadata_row_type\": \"base_item\",\n        \"metadata_item_id\": item[\"item_id\"],\n        \"metadata_item_source\": item[\"item_source\"],\n        \"metadata_numeric_range\": item[\"numeric_range\"],\n        \"metadata_chain_length\": item[\"chain_length\"],\n        \"metadata_trace\": item[\"trace\"],\n        \"metadata_final_answer\": item[\"final_answer\"],\n        \"metadata_fold\": fold,\n    }\n\n\ndef variant_to_row(variant, fold: int):\n    return {\n        \"input\": render_corrupted_trace(variant[\"problem_text\"], variant[\"trace\"], variant[\"full_corrupted_trace\"]),\n        \"output\": f\"{variant['label']}|correct_final_answer={variant['trace'][-1]['result']}\",\n        \"metadata_row_type\": \"error_variant\",\n        \"metadata_base_item_id\": variant[\"base_item_id\"],\n        \"metadata_error_type\": variant[\"error_type\"],\n        \"metadata_injected_step_index\": variant[\"injected_step_index\"],\n        \"metadata_original_step\": variant[\"original_step\"],\n        \"metadata_corrupted_step\": variant[\"corrupted_step\"],\n        \"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\": variant[\"corrupted_final_answer\"],\n        \"metadata_mod9_residue_preserved\": variant[\"mod9_residue_preserved\"],\n        \"metadata_label\": variant[\"label\"],\n        \"metadata_item_source\": variant[\"item_source\"],\n        \"metadata_numeric_range\": variant[\"numeric_range\"],\n        \"metadata_chain_length\": variant[\"chain_length\"],\n        \"metadata_fold\": fold,\n    }\n\n\ndef assign_fold(rng: random.Random) -> int:\n    \"\"\"80/20 split encoded as fold 0 (train) / 1 (test).\"\"\"\n    return 1 if rng.random() < 0.2 else 0\n\n\ndef build_dataset_group(name: str, base_items: list[dict]):\n    rows = []\n    skip_log: list[tuple[str, str]] = []\n    for item in base_items:\n        fold = assign_fold(RNG)\n        rows.append(base_item_to_row(item, fold))\n        for variant in make_error_variants(item, skip_log):\n            rows.append(variant_to_row(variant, fold))\n    if skip_log:\n        logger.info(f\"[{name}] {len(skip_log)} error-injection skips (structurally inapplicable): sample={skip_log[:5]}\")\n    n_base = sum(1 for r in rows if r[\"metadata_row_type\"] == \"base_item\")\n    n_variant = sum(1 for r in rows if r[\"metadata_row_type\"] == \"error_variant\")\n    logger.info(f\"[{name}] {n_base} base rows + {n_variant} error-variant rows = {len(rows)} total\")\n    return rows\n\n\ndef main():\n    logger.info(\"Loading & extracting GSM8K real base items\")\n    gsm8k_items = load_gsm8k_base_items(max_per_cell=20)\n\n    logger.info(\"Generating synthetic template base items\")\n    synthetic_items = generate_synthetic_items(per_cell=20)\n\n    logger.info(\"Building gsm8k_real rows (base + error variants)\")\n    gsm8k_rows = build_dataset_group(\"gsm8k_real\", gsm8k_items)\n\n    logger.info(\"Building synthetic_template rows (base + error variants)\")\n    synthetic_rows = build_dataset_group(\"synthetic_template\", synthetic_items)\n\n    # Single combined dataset per the artifact plan (target_num_datasets: 1):\n    # gsm8k_real + synthetic_template are complementary components of one\n    # arithmetic-checksum benchmark, not separate candidate datasets.\n    all_rows = gsm8k_rows + synthetic_rows\n    out = {\n        \"metadata\": {\n            \"source\": \"openai/gsm8k (main config, train+test) + procedural synthetic template generator\",\n            \"description\": \"Multi-step arithmetic word problems with computation traces, plus deterministic error-injection variants labeled by mod-9 checksum detectability.\",\n        },\n        \"datasets\": [\n            {\"dataset\": \"arithmetic_checksum_dataset\", \"examples\": all_rows},\n        ],\n    }\n    OUT_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=2))\n    logger.info(f\"Wrote {OUT_PATH} ({OUT_PATH.stat().st_size / 1e6:.2f} MB), {len(all_rows)} total rows\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n", "numLines": 634, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 634}}

b, \"*\": a * b, \"/\": round(a / b, 6) if b else None}[op]\n\n\ndef mod9_residue(n):\n    n = abs(round(n))\n    return 0 if n == 0 else (9 if n % 9 == 0 else n % 9)\n\n\ndef verify_base_row(row):\n    trace = row[\"metadata_trace\"]\n    for i, s in enumerate(trace):\n        expect = apply_op(s[\"operand_1\"], s[\"operation\"], s[\"operand_2\"])\n        if expect is None or abs(expect - s[\"result\"]) > max(EPS, abs(expect) * 1e-4):\n            return f\"step {i} arithmetic mismatch: {s['operand_1']}{s['operation']}{s['operand_2']} != {s['result']}\"\n    if abs(trace[-1][\"result\"] - float(row[\"output\"])) > max(EPS, abs(trace[-1][\"result\"]) * 1e-4):\n        return f\"final trace result {trace[-1]['result']} != declared output {row['output']}\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef verify_variant_row(row):\n    parsed_steps = [(int(i), float(o1), op, float(o2), float(r)) for i, o1, op, o2, r in STEP_RE.findall(row[\"input\"])]\n    if not parsed_steps:\n        return \"could not parse any Step lines from rendered input\"\n\n    injected_idx = row[\"metadata_injected_step_index\"]\n    for idx, o1, op, o2, r in parsed_steps:\n        expect = apply_op(o1, op, o2)\n        matches = expect is not None and abs(expect - r) <= max(EPS, abs(expect) * 1e-4)\n        if idx == injected_idx:\n            if matches:\n                return f\"injected step {idx} is arithmetically CORRECT ({o1}{op}{o2}={r}) — not actually a corruption\"\n        else:\n            if not matches:\n                return f\"non-injected step {idx} arithmetic mismatch: {o1}{op}{o2} != {r} (expected {expect}) — corruption should only affect step {injected_idx} and its downstream propagation, not break arithmetic elsewhere\"\n\n    rendered_final = parsed_steps[-1][4]\n    corrupted_final = row[\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\"]\n    if abs(rendered_final - corrupted_final) > max(EPS, abs(rendered_final) * 1e-4):\n        return f\"rendered final step result {rendered_final} != metadata_corrupted_final_answer {corrupted_final}\"\n\n    label = row[\"metadata_label\"]\n    correct_final_str = row[\"output\"].split(\"correct_final_answer=\")[-1]\n    correct_final = float(correct_final_str)\n    residue_correct = mod9_residue(correct_final)\n    residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\n    preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\n    expected_label = \"checksum_invisible\" if preserved else \"checksum_detectable\"\n    if label != expected_label:\n        return f\"label {label} != recomputed {expected_label} (residues {residue_correct} vs {residue_corrupted})\"\n    if row[\"metadata_mod9_residue_preserved\"] != preserved:\n        return \"metadata_mod9_residue_preserved disagrees with recomputed residue check\"\n    if abs(corrupted_final - rendered_final) > EPS:\n        return \"corrupted_final_answer doesn't match trace\"\n    if abs(corrupted_final - correct_final) < EPS:\n        return \"corrupted_final_answer equals correct_final_answer (no-op corruption)\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef main():\n    data = json.loads(Path(\"full_data_out.json\").read_text())\n    total, failures = 0, []\n    by_source: dict[str, list] = {}\n    for row in data[\"datasets\"][0][\"examples\"]:\n        by_source.setdefault(row[\"metadata_item_source\"], []).append(row)\n    groups = [{\"dataset\": src, \"examples\": rows} for src, rows in by_source.items()]\n    for group in groups:\n        n_base = n_variant = n_base_fail = n_variant_fail = 0\n        for row in group[\"examples\"]:\n            total += 1\n            if row[\"metadata_row_type\"] == \"base_item\":\n                n_base += 1\n                err = verify_base_row(row)\n                if err:\n                    n_base_fail += 1\n                    failures.append((group[\"dataset\"], row.get(\"metadata_item_id\"), \"base\", err))\n            else:\n                n_variant += 1\n                err = verify_variant_row(row)\n                if err:\n                    n_variant_fail += 1\n                    failures.append((group[\"dataset\"], row.get(\"metadata_base_item_id\"), row.get(\"metadata_error_type\"), err))\n        logger.info(f\"[{group['dataset']}] base: {n_base} checked, {n_base_fail} failed | variants: {n_variant} checked, {n_variant_fail} failed\")\n\n    logger.info(f\"TOTAL rows checked: {total}, TOTAL failures: {len(failures)}\")\n    for f in failures[:30]:\n        logger.error(f\"FAIL: {f}\")\n    if failures:\n        raise SystemExit(f\"{len(failures)} verification failures\")\n    logger.info(\"ALL ROWS VERIFIED CONSISTENT\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n", "numLines": 118, "startLine": 1, "totalLines": 118}}

import json, random
src = '/home/adrian/projects/ai-inventor/aii_data/users/uitest-20260731/runs/run_0WmBa7GFLIzI/3_invention_loop/iter_1/gen_art/gen_art_dataset_1/full_data_out.json'
d = json.load(open(src))
rows = d['datasets'][0]['examples']
rng = random.Random(42)

base_by_id = {r['metadata_item_id']: r for r in rows if r['metadata_row_type']=='base_item'}
variants = [r for r in rows if r['metadata_row_type']=='error_variant']

# pick base items covering both sources and small/large range, chain_length spread
picked_base_ids = set()
selected_bases = []
by_key = {}
for r in base_by_id.values():
    key = (r['metadata_item_source'], r['metadata_numeric_range'])
    by_key.setdefault(key, []).append(r)
for key, items in by_key.items():
    rng.shuffle(items)
    for it in items[:5]:
        selected_bases.append(it)
        picked_base_ids.add(it['metadata_item_id'])

# pick variants: for each error_type x label combo, a few examples, restricted to selected base ids first, else any
combos = {}
for v in variants:
    combos.setdefault((v['metadata_error_type'], v['metadata_label']), []).append(v)

selected_variants = []
for combo, items in combos.items():
    rng.shuffle(items)
    # prefer ones whose base is in selected_bases
    preferred = [v for v in items if v['metadata_base_item_id'] in picked_base_ids]
    pool = preferred if len(preferred)>=3 else items
    selected_variants.extend(pool[:4])

# ensure bases for all selected variants included
extra_base_ids = set(v['metadata_base_item_id'] for v in selected_variants) - picked_base_ids
for bid in extra_base_ids:
    selected_bases.append(base_by_id[bid])
    picked_base_ids.add(bid)

all_selected = selected_bases + selected_variants
print(len(selected_bases), len(selected_variants), len(all_selected))

out = {
    'metadata': d['metadata'],
    'datasets': [{'dataset': 'arithmetic_checksum_dataset', 'examples': all_selected}]
}
json.dump(out, open('mini_demo_data.json','w'), indent=2)
import os
print('size', os.path.getsize('mini_demo_data.json'))
"

problems (from GSM8K + a procedural generator) paired with explicit computation traces, plus deterministically-corrupted \\\\\\\"error variant\\\\\\\" traces.\\\\n\\\\nEach error variant is labeled by a cheap **mod-9 digit-checksum**: if the corrupted final answer's mod-9 residue differs from the correct answer's residue, the corruption is `checksum_detectable`; if the residue is coincidentally preserved, it's `checksum_invisible`.\\\\n\\\\nThis notebook reproduces the mod-9 self-critique check from `data.py`/`verify.py` directly on the raw traces (not just trusting the precomputed labels), {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"2e942de6\",\n   \"source\": \"## Config\\\\n\\\\nTunable parameters for this demo. `MOD_BASE` is the checksum modulus used by the mod-9 digit-checksum self-critique rule (`data.py`'s `mod9_residue`). `N_PREVIEW_ROWS` controls how many example rows are printed below.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"51319716\",\n   \"source\": \"MOD_BASE = 9  # digit-root checksum modulus (mod-9 casting-out-nines)\\\\nN_PREVIEW_ROWS = 2  # how many raw rows to print for inspection\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"e27a44a7\",\n   \"source\": \"## Preview a base item and an error variant\\\\n\\\\nEach `base_item` row is a clean word problem with its full step-by-step arithmetic trace. Each `error_variant` row corrupts one step of a base item's trace (one of `digit_transposition`, `dropped_carry`, `sign_flip`, `wrong_operand_substitution`), propagates the corruption through every downstream step, and renders the corrupted trace as text — this is what a model would see if it were asked to spot-check its own reasoning.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"127c2a9c\",\n   \"source\": \"base_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"base_item\\\\\\\"]\\\\nvariant_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"error_variant\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\nfor r in base_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"BASE ITEM:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| chain_length:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_chain_length\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"ERROR VARIANT:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_base_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| error_type:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| label:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"c0352dc5\",\n   \"source\": \"## The mod-9 checksum self-critique rule\\\\n\\\\nThis is the exact `mod9_residue` digit-root check used by `data.py` to generate the dataset's `checksum_detectable` / `checksum_invisible` labels, and independently re-derived by `verify.py`. A cheap self-critique step recomputes this residue for the corrupted final answer and compares it against the residue of the stated correct answer: if the residues differ, the checksum flags the error as detectable; if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n A cheap self-critique step recomputes this residue for the corrupted final answer and compares it against the residue of the stated correct answer: if the residues differ, the checksum flags the error as detectable; if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"d366c9a8\",\n   \"source\": \"def mod9_residue(n) -> int:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\\\\n    if n == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    r = n % MOD_BASE\\\\n    return MOD_BASE if r == 0 else r\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef self_critique_flag(row):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Recompute the mod-9 checksum self-critique verdict for one error_variant row.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    correct_final = float(row[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"].split(\\\\\\\"correct_final_answer=\\\\\\\")[-1])\\\\n    corrupted_final = row[\\\\\\\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    residue_correct = mod9_residue(correct_final)\\\\n    residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\\\\n    preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\\\\n    return \\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\" if preserved else \\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n  },\n  \"language_info\": {\n   \"name\": \"python\",\n   \"version\": \"3.12.12\"\n  }\n },\n \"nbformat\": 4,\n \"nbformat_minor\": 5\n}"}

4,\n \"nbformat_minor\": 5\n}", "updated_file": "{\n \"cells\": [\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": \"# Arithmetic Problems with Checksum Error Labels\\\\n\\\\nThis demo explores the **`arithmetic_checksum_dataset`**: multi-step arithmetic word problems (from GSM8K + a procedural generator) paired with explicit computation traces, plus deterministically-corrupted \\\\\\\"error variant\\\\\\\" traces.\\\\n\\\\nEach error variant is labeled by a cheap **mod-9 digit-checksum**: if the corrupted final answer's mod-9 residue differs from the correct answer's residue, the corruption is `checksum_detectable`; if the residue is coincidentally preserved, it's `checksum_invisible`.\\\\n\\\\nThis notebook reproduces the mod-9 self-critique check from `data.py`/`verify.py` directly on the raw traces (not just trusting the precomputed labels), and measures how often this cheap self-critique step would catch an injected arithmetic error — motivating the research question of whether a short self-critique step improves accuracy on multi-step arithmetic word problems.\\\"\",\n   \"id\": \"cell-0\"\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"e146ad32\",\n   \"source\": \"import json\\\\nimport os\\\\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\\\\n\\\\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"53acc980\",\n   \"source\": \"GITHUB_DATA_URL = \\\\\\\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AMGrobelnik/ai-invention-74dec2-checksum-self-critique-helps-weak-arithm/main/round-1/dataset-1/demo/mini_demo_data.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\ndef load_data():\\\\n    try:\\\\n        import urllib.request\\\\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(GITHUB_DATA_URL) as response:\\\\n            return json.loads(response.read().decode())\\\\n    except Exception: pass\\\\n    if os.path.exists(\\\\\\\"mini_demo_data.json\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        with open(\\\\\\\"mini_demo_data.json\\\\\\\") as f: return json.load(f)\\\\n    raise \"f519355f\",\n   \"source\": \"data = load_data()\\\\nrows = data[\\\\\\\"datasets\\\\\\\"][0][\\\\\\\"examples\\\\\\\"]\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"Loaded {len(rows)} rows from '{data['datasets'][0]['dataset']}'\\\\\\\")\\\\nprint(Counter(r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] for r in rows))\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"2e942de6\",\n   \"source\": \"## Config\\\\n\\\\nTunable parameters for this demo. `MOD_BASE` is the checksum modulus used by the mod-9 digit-checksum self-critique rule (`data.py`'s `mod9_residue`). `N_PREVIEW_ROWS` controls how many example rows are printed below.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"51319716\",\n   \"source\": \"MOD_BASE = 9  # digit-root checksum modulus (mod-9 casting-out-nines)\\\\nN_PREVIEW_ROWS = 2  # how many raw rows to print for inspection\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"e27a44a7\",\n   \"source\": \"## Preview a base item and an error variant\\\\n\\\\nEach `base_item` row is a clean word problem with its full step-by-step arithmetic trace. Each `error_variant` row corrupts one step of a base item's trace (one of `digit_transposition`, `dropped_carry`, `sign_flip`, `wrong_operand_substitution`), propagates the corruption through every downstream step, and renders the corrupted trace as text — this is what a model would see if it were asked to spot-check its own reasoning.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"127c2a9c\",\n   \"source\": \"base_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"base_item\\\\\\\"]\\\\nvariant_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"error_variant\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\nfor r in base_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"BASE ITEM:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| chain_length:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_chain_length\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"ERROR VARIANT:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_base_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| error_type:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| label:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"c0352dc5\",\n   \"source\": \"## The mod-9 checksum self-critique rule\\\\n\\\\nThis is the exact `mod9_residue` digit-root check used by `data.py` to generate the dataset's `checksum_detectable` / `checksum_invisible` labels, and independently re-derived by `verify.py`. A cheap self-critique step recomputes this residue for the corrupted final answer and compares it against the residue of the stated correct answer: if the residues differ, the checksum flags the error as detectable; if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"d366c9a8\",\n   \"source\": \"def mod9_residue(n) -> int:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\\\\n    if n == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    r = n % MOD_BASE\\\\n    return MOD_BASE if r == 0 else r\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef self_critique_flag(row):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Recompute the mod-9 checksum self-critique verdict for one error_variant row.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    correct_final = float(row[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"].split(\\\\\\\"correct_final_answer=\\\\\\\")[-1])\\\\n    corrupted_final = row[\\\\\\\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    residue_correct = mod9_residue(correct_final)\\\\n    residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\\\\n    preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\\\\n    return \\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\" if preserved else \\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n  },\n  \"language_info\": {\n   \"name\": \"python\",\n   \"version\": \"3.12.12\"\n  }\n },\n \"nbformat\": 4,\n \"nbformat_minor\": 5\n}"}

if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"d366c9a8\",\n   \"source\": \"def mod9_residue(n) -> int:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\\\\n    if n == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    r = n % MOD_BASE\\\\n    return MOD_BASE if r == 0 else r\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef self_critique_flag(row):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Recompute the mod-9 checksum self-critique verdict for one error_variant row.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    correct_final = float(row[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"].split(\\\\\\\"correct_final_answer=\\\\\\\")[-1])\\\\n    corrupted_final = row[\\\\\\\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    residue_correct = mod9_residue(correct_final)\\\\n    residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\\\\n    preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\\\\n    return \\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\" if preserved else \\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"a1f5b961\",\n   \"source\": \"## Run the self-critique check over every error variant\\\\n\\\\nFor each corrupted-trace row we recompute the checksum verdict from scratch and compare it against the dataset's stored `metadata_label`, then break results down by `error_type` — this shows which corruption types the mod-9 checksum reliably catches and which ones it systematically misses (the `checksum_invisible` cases).\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n  },\n  \"language_info\": {\n   \"name\": \"python\",\n   \"version\": \"3.12.12\"\n  }\n },\n \"nbformat\": 4,\n \"nbformat_minor\": 5\n}"}

{\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"2e942de6\",\n   \"source\": \"## Config\\\\n\\\\nTunable parameters for this demo. `MOD_BASE` is the checksum modulus used by the mod-9 digit-checksum self-critique rule (`data.py`'s `mod9_residue`). `N_PREVIEW_ROWS` controls how many example rows are printed below.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"51319716\",\n   \"source\": \"MOD_BASE = 9  # digit-root checksum modulus (mod-9 casting-out-nines)\\\\nN_PREVIEW_ROWS = 2  # how many raw rows to print for inspection\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"e27a44a7\",\n   \"source\": \"## Preview a base item and an error variant\\\\n\\\\nEach `base_item` row is a clean word problem with its full step-by-step arithmetic trace. Each `error_variant` row corrupts one step of a base item's trace (one of `digit_transposition`, `dropped_carry`, `sign_flip`, `wrong_operand_substitution`), propagates the corruption through every downstream step, and renders the corrupted trace as text — this is what a model would see if it were asked to spot-check its own reasoning.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"127c2a9c\",\n   \"source\": \"base_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"base_item\\\\\\\"]\\\\nvariant_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"error_variant\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\nfor r in base_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"BASE ITEM:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| chain_length:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_chain_length\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"ERROR VARIANT:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_base_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| error_type:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| label:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"c0352dc5\",\n   \"source\": \"## The mod-9 checksum self-critique rule\\\\n\\\\nThis is the exact `mod9_residue` digit-root check used by `data.py` to generate the dataset's `checksum_detectable` / `checksum_invisible` labels, and independently re-derived by `verify.py`. A cheap self-critique step recomputes this residue for the corrupted final answer and compares it against the residue of the stated correct answer: if the residues differ, the checksum flags the error as detectable; if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"d366c9a8\",\n   \"source\": \"def mod9_residue(n) -> int:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\\\\n    if n == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    r = n % MOD_BASE\\\\n    return MOD_BASE if r == 0 else r\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef self_critique_flag(row):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Recompute the mod-9 checksum self-critique verdict for one error_variant row.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    correct_final = float(row[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"].split(\\\\\\\"correct_final_answer=\\\\\\\")[-1])\\\\n    corrupted_final = row[\\\\\\\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    residue_correct = mod9_residue(correct_final)\\\\n    residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\\\\n    preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\\\\n    return \\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\" if preserved else \\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"a1f5b961\",\n   \"source\": \"## Run the self-critique check over every error variant\\\\n\\\\nFor each corrupted-trace row we recompute the checksum verdict from scratch and compare it against the dataset's stored `metadata_label`, then break results down by `error_type` — this shows which corruption types the mod-9 checksum reliably catches and which ones it systematically misses (the `checksum_invisible` cases).\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"f4a5fa46\",\n   \"source\": \"n_agree = 0\\\\nby_error_type = defaultdict(lambda: Counter())\\\\nlabel_counts = Counter()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows:\\\\n    recomputed = self_critique_flag(r)\\\\n    stored = r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    agree = recomputed == stored\\\\n    n_agree += agree\\\\n    by_error_type[r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"]][recomputed] += 1\\\\n    label_counts[recomputed] += 1\\\\n\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"Recomputed checksum verdict agrees with stored metadata_label on {n_agree}/{len(variant_rows)} variants\\\\\\\")\\\\nprint(\\\\\\\"Verdict counts:\\\\\\\", dict(label_counts))\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n  },\n  \"language_info\": {\n   \"name\": problems (from GSM8K + a procedural generator) paired with explicit computation traces, plus deterministically-corrupted \\\\\\\"error variant\\\\\\\" traces.\\\\n\\\\nEach error variant is labeled by a cheap **mod-9 digit-checksum**: if the corrupted final answer's mod-9 residue differs from the correct answer's residue, the corruption is `checksum_detectable`; if the residue is coincidentally preserved, it's `checksum_invisible`.\\\\n\\\\nThis notebook reproduces the mod-9 self-critique check from `data.py`/`verify.py` directly on the raw traces (not just trusting the precomputed labels), and measures how often this cheap self-critique step would catch an injected arithmetic error — motivating the research question of whether a short self-critique step improves accuracy on multi-step arithmetic word problems.\\\"\",\n   \"id\": \"cell-0\"\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"236df681\",\n   \"source\": \"import subprocess, sys\\\\ndef _pip(*a): {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"2e942de6\",\n   \"source\": \"## Config\\\\n\\\\nTunable parameters for this demo. `MOD_BASE` is the checksum modulus used by the mod-9 digit-checksum self-critique rule (`data.py`'s `mod9_residue`). `N_PREVIEW_ROWS` controls how many example rows are printed below.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"51319716\",\n   \"source\": \"MOD_BASE = 9  # digit-root checksum modulus (mod-9 casting-out-nines)\\\\nN_PREVIEW_ROWS = 2  # how many raw rows to print for inspection\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"e27a44a7\",\n   \"source\": \"## Preview a base item and an error variant\\\\n\\\\nEach `base_item` row is a clean word problem with its full step-by-step arithmetic trace. Each `error_variant` row corrupts one step of a base item's trace (one of `digit_transposition`, `dropped_carry`, `sign_flip`, `wrong_operand_substitution`), propagates the corruption through every downstream step, and renders the corrupted trace as text — this is what a model would see if it were asked to spot-check its own reasoning.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"127c2a9c\",\n   \"source\": \"base_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"base_item\\\\\\\"]\\\\nvariant_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"error_variant\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\nfor r in base_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"BASE ITEM:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| chain_length:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_chain_length\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"ERROR VARIANT:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_base_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| error_type:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| label:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"c0352dc5\",\n   \"source\": \"## The mod-9 checksum self-critique rule\\\\n\\\\nThis is the exact `mod9_residue` digit-root check used by `data.py` to generate the dataset's `checksum_detectable` / `checksum_invisible` labels, and independently re-derived by `verify.py`. A cheap self-critique step recomputes this residue for the corrupted final answer and compares it against the residue of the stated correct answer: if the residues differ, the checksum flags the error as detectable; if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"d366c9a8\",\n   \"source\": \"def mod9_residue(n) -> int:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\\\\n    if n == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    r = n % MOD_BASE\\\\n    return MOD_BASE if r == 0 else r\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef self_critique_flag(row):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Recompute the mod-9 checksum self-critique verdict for one error_variant row.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    correct_final = float(row[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"].split(\\\\\\\"correct_final_answer=\\\\\\\")[-1])\\\\n    corrupted_final = row[\\\\\\\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    residue_correct = preserved else \\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"a1f5b961\",\n   \"source\": \"## Run the self-critique check over every error variant\\\\n\\\\nFor each corrupted-trace row we recompute the checksum verdict from scratch and compare it against the dataset's stored Counter()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows:\\\\n    recomputed = self_critique_flag(r)\\\\n    stored = r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    agree = recomputed == stored\\\\n    n_agree += \"source\": \"import json\\\\nimport os\\\\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\\\\n\\\\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"53acc980\",\n   \"source\": \"GITHUB_DATA_URL = \\\\\\\"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AMGrobelnik/ai-invention-74dec2-checksum-self-critique-helps-weak-arithm/main/round-1/dataset-1/demo/mini_demo_data.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\ndef load_data():\\\\n    try:\\\\n        import urllib.request\\\\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(GITHUB_DATA_URL) as response:\\\\n            return json.loads(response.read().decode())\\\\n    except Exception: pass\\\\n    if os.path.exists(\\\\\\\"mini_demo_data.json\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        with open(\\\\\\\"mini_demo_data.json\\\\\\\") as f: return json.load(f)\\\\n    raise FileNotFoundError(\\\\\\\"Could not load mini_demo_data.json\\\\\\\")\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"f519355f\",\n   \"source\": \"data = load_data()\\\\nrows = data[\\\\\\\"datasets\\\\\\\"][0][\\\\\\\"examples\\\\\\\"]\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"Loaded {len(rows)} rows from '{data['datasets'][0]['dataset']}'\\\\\\\")\\\\nprint(Counter(r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] for r in rows))\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"2e942de6\",\n   \"source\": \"## Config\\\\n\\\\nTunable parameters for this demo. `MOD_BASE` is the checksum modulus used by the mod-9 digit-checksum self-critique rule (`data.py`'s `mod9_residue`). `N_PREVIEW_ROWS` controls how many example rows are printed below.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"51319716\",\n   \"source\": \"MOD_BASE = 9  # digit-root checksum modulus (mod-9 casting-out-nines)\\\\nN_PREVIEW_ROWS = 2  # how many raw rows to print for inspection\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"e27a44a7\",\n   \"source\": \"## Preview a base item and an error variant\\\\n\\\\nEach `base_item` row is a clean word problem with its full step-by-step arithmetic trace. Each `error_variant` row corrupts one step of a base item's trace (one of `digit_transposition`, `dropped_carry`, `sign_flip`, `wrong_operand_substitution`), propagates the corruption through every downstream step, and renders the corrupted trace as text — this is what a model would see if it were asked to spot-check its own reasoning.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"127c2a9c\",\n   \"source\": \"base_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"base_item\\\\\\\"]\\\\nvariant_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"error_variant\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\nfor r in base_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"BASE ITEM:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| chain_length:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_chain_length\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"ERROR VARIANT:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_base_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| error_type:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| label:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"c0352dc5\",\n   \"source\": \"## The mod-9 checksum self-critique rule\\\\n\\\\nThis is the exact `mod9_residue` digit-root check used by `data.py` to generate the dataset's `checksum_detectable` / `checksum_invisible` labels, and independently re-derived by `verify.py`. A cheap self-critique step recomputes this residue for the corrupted final answer and compares it against the residue of the stated correct answer: if the residues differ, the checksum flags the error as detectable; if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"d366c9a8\",\n   \"source\": \"def mod9_residue(n) -> int:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\\\\n    if n == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    r = n % MOD_BASE\\\\n    return MOD_BASE if r == 0 else r\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef self_critique_flag(row):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Recompute the mod-9 checksum self-critique verdict for one error_variant row.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    correct_final = float(row[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"].split(\\\\\\\"correct_final_answer=\\\\\\\")[-1])\\\\n    corrupted_final = row[\\\\\\\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    residue_correct = mod9_residue(correct_final)\\\\n    residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\\\\n    preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\\\\n    return \\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\" if preserved else \\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"a1f5b961\",\n   \"source\": \"## Run the self-critique check over every error variant\\\\n\\\\nFor each corrupted-trace row we recompute the checksum verdict from scratch and compare it against the dataset's stored `metadata_label`, then break results down by `error_type` — this shows which corruption types the mod-9 checksum reliably catches and which ones it systematically misses (the `checksum_invisible` cases).\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"f4a5fa46\",\n   \"source\": \"n_agree = 0\\\\nby_error_type = defaultdict(lambda: Counter())\\\\nlabel_counts = Counter()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows:\\\\n    recomputed = self_critique_flag(r)\\\\n    stored = r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    agree = recomputed == stored\\\\n    n_agree += agree\\\\n    by_error_type[r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"]][recomputed] += 1\\\\n    label_counts[recomputed] += 1\\\\n\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"Recomputed checksum verdict agrees with stored metadata_label on {n_agree}/{len(variant_rows)} variants\\\\\\\")\\\\nprint(\\\\\\\"Verdict counts:\\\\\\\", dict(label_counts))\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"457d130f\",\n   \"source\": \"## Results\\\\n\\\\nDetection rate of the mod-9 self-critique checksum, broken down by injected error type.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"ecf75835\",\n   \"source\": \"error_types = sorted(by_error_type.keys())\\\\ndetectable_rates = [\\\\n    by_error_type[et][\\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"] / sum(by_error_type[et].values()) for et in error_types\\\\n]\\\\n\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"{'error_type':<28}{'detectable':>12}{'invisible':>12}{'detect_rate':>14}\\\\\\\")\\\\nfor et in error_types:\\\\n    d = by_error_type[et][\\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    inv = by_error_type[et][\\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    total = d + inv\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"{et:<28}{d:>12}{inv:>12}{d / total:>14.1%}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7, 4))\\\\nax.bar(error_types, detectable_rates, color=\\\\\\\"#4C72B0\\\\\\\")\\\\nax.set_ylim(0, 1)\\\\nax.set_ylabel(\\\\\\\"checksum detection rate\\\\\\\")\\\\nax.set_title(\\\\\\\"Mod-9 self-critique checksum: detection rate by injected error type\\\\\\\")\\\\nplt.xticks(rotation=20, ha=\\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\")\\\\nplt.tight_layout()\\\\nplt.show()\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n  },\n  \"language_info\": {\n   \"name\": \"python\",\n   \"version\": \"3.12.12\"\n  }\n },\n \"nbformat\": {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"2e942de6\",\n   \"source\": \"## Config\\\\n\\\\nTunable parameters for this demo. `MOD_BASE` is the checksum modulus used by the mod-9 digit-checksum self-critique rule (`data.py`'s `mod9_residue`). `N_PREVIEW_ROWS` controls how many example rows are printed below.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"51319716\",\n   \"source\": \"MOD_BASE = 9  # digit-root checksum modulus (mod-9 casting-out-nines)\\\\nN_PREVIEW_ROWS = 2  # how many raw rows to print for inspection\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"e27a44a7\",\n   \"source\": \"## Preview a base item and an error variant\\\\n\\\\nEach `base_item` row is a clean word problem with its full step-by-step arithmetic trace. Each `error_variant` row corrupts one step of a base item's trace (one of `digit_transposition`, `dropped_carry`, `sign_flip`, `wrong_operand_substitution`), propagates the corruption through every downstream step, and renders the corrupted trace as text — this is what a model would see if it were asked to spot-check its own reasoning.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"127c2a9c\",\n   \"source\": \"base_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"base_item\\\\\\\"]\\\\nvariant_rows = [r for r in rows if r[\\\\\\\"metadata_row_type\\\\\\\"] == \\\\\\\"error_variant\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\nfor r in base_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"BASE ITEM:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| chain_length:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_chain_length\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows[:N_PREVIEW_ROWS]:\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"ERROR VARIANT:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_base_item_id\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| error_type:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"| label:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(r[\\\\\\\"input\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"-> output:\\\\\\\", r[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    print()\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"c0352dc5\",\n   \"source\": \"## The mod-9 checksum self-critique rule\\\\n\\\\nThis is the exact `mod9_residue` digit-root check used by `data.py` to generate the dataset's `checksum_detectable` / `checksum_invisible` labels, and independently re-derived by `verify.py`. A cheap self-critique step recomputes this residue for the corrupted final answer and compares it against the residue of the stated correct answer: if the residues differ, the checksum flags the error as detectable; if they coincidentally match, the error is invisible to this check.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"d366c9a8\",\n   \"source\": \"def mod9_residue(n) -> int:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Digit root of |n| via mod-9, mapping 0 residue to 9 (except n==0 -> 0).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = abs(round(n)) if isinstance(n, float) else abs(n)\\\\n    if n == 0:\\\\n        return 0\\\\n    r = n % MOD_BASE\\\\n    return MOD_BASE if r == 0 else r\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef self_critique_flag(row):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Recompute the mod-9 checksum self-critique verdict for one error_variant row.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    correct_final = float(row[\\\\\\\"output\\\\\\\"].split(\\\\\\\"correct_final_answer=\\\\\\\")[-1])\\\\n    corrupted_final = row[\\\\\\\"metadata_corrupted_final_answer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    residue_correct = mod9_residue(correct_final)\\\\n    residue_corrupted = mod9_residue(corrupted_final)\\\\n    preserved = residue_correct == residue_corrupted\\\\n    return \\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\" if preserved else \\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"a1f5b961\",\n   \"source\": \"## Run the self-critique check over every error variant\\\\n\\\\nFor each corrupted-trace row we recompute the checksum verdict from scratch and compare it against the dataset's stored `metadata_label`, then break results down by `error_type` — this shows which corruption types the mod-9 checksum reliably catches and which ones it systematically misses (the `checksum_invisible` cases).\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"f4a5fa46\",\n   \"source\": \"n_agree = 0\\\\nby_error_type = defaultdict(lambda: Counter())\\\\nlabel_counts = Counter()\\\\n\\\\nfor r in variant_rows:\\\\n    recomputed = self_critique_flag(r)\\\\n    stored = r[\\\\\\\"metadata_label\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    agree = recomputed == stored\\\\n    n_agree += agree\\\\n    by_error_type[r[\\\\\\\"metadata_error_type\\\\\\\"]][recomputed] += 1\\\\n    label_counts[recomputed] += 1\\\\n\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"Recomputed checksum verdict agrees with stored metadata_label on {n_agree}/{len(variant_rows)} variants\\\\\\\")\\\\nprint(\\\\\\\"Verdict counts:\\\\\\\", dict(label_counts))\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"457d130f\",\n   \"source\": \"## Results\\\\n\\\\nDetection rate of the mod-9 self-critique checksum, broken down by injected error type.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"ecf75835\",\n   \"source\": \"error_types = sorted(by_error_type.keys())\\\\ndetectable_rates = [\\\\n    by_error_type[et][\\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"] / sum(by_error_type[et].values()) for et in error_types\\\\n]\\\\n\\\\nprint(f\\\\\\\"{'error_type':<28}{'detectable':>12}{'invisible':>12}{'detect_rate':>14}\\\\\\\")\\\\nfor et in error_types:\\\\n    d = by_error_type[et][\\\\\\\"checksum_detectable\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    inv = by_error_type[et][\\\\\\\"checksum_invisible\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    total = d + inv\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"{et:<28}{d:>12}{inv:>12}{d / total:>14.1%}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7, 4))\\\\nax.bar(error_types, detectable_rates, color=\\\\\\\"#4C72B0\\\\\\\")\\\\nax.set_ylim(0, 1)\\\\nax.set_ylabel(\\\\\\\"checksum detection rate\\\\\\\")\\\\nax.set_title(\\\\\\\"Mod-9 self-critique checksum: detection rate by injected error type\\\\\\\")\\\\nplt.xticks(rotation=20, ha=\\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\")\\\\nplt.tight_layout()\\\\nplt.show()\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n
